STRUCTURALLY CONFIRMED UNCHANGED at HEAD 2026-10-02: packages/operator-core/lib/fleet-logs-gc.ts discoverFleetLogsDirs() (L88-102) still returns only ~/.papercusp/fleet-logs and <workspacesRoot>/*/.papercusp/fleet-logs, and listFilesWithStat is isFile()-only with no recursion, so it cannot see any sibling directory regardless of size. storage/disk-space-alarm.ts (statfs tripwire) detects LOW FREE SPACE but not WHO is consuming it. Original measurement 2026-09-20: ~/.papercusp reached 279 GB with fleet-logs contributing nothing; consumers were ownerless one-off dirs (mktemp-suffixed byoc-p318-release.* 82.8G, p309-accept.* , p302-vmrelease-audit.*, six near-identical p046-r*-publication snapshots 6.6G each, su-codex-homes 29.3G, pot-git 12.5G, bench-harnesses, live-fed-gate), starving the green-checkpoint disk preflight to ~4.4 GiB over its 37.51 GiB critical reserve. NOT re-measured today (a du over 279 GB would itself load the box): step 1 of this item is to measure current top-level sizes cheaply (du --max-depth=1 with a timeout, or storage:usage). SCOPE (safe by construction, delete nothing owned): (1) add a size-attribution report to the disk alarm so a trip names the top N children of ~/.papercusp and workspacesRoot by bytes, not just free space; (2) add a reaper ONLY for directories matching an explicit allowlist of ownerless temp patterns (mktemp-style .XXXXXX suffix) older than a configurable age with no process holding them open (lsof/fuser or the task-manager ledger), dry-run by default, never-throws like fleet-logs-gc; (3) NEVER auto-delete su-codex-homes, pot-git, bench-harnesses or any dir that is not allowlisted — those get reported only. RECURRENCE GUARD (required): unit tests with a temp-dir fixture tree asserting (a) the attribution names the large sibling that fleet-logs-gc ignores, (b) the reaper deletes an aged mktemp-suffixed dir, (c) it refuses a non-allowlisted large dir and a dir with an open handle, (d) dry-run deletes nothing; has token-budget discipline for LLM spend but zero equivalent discipline for disk scratch. The result is that 2.3TB of papercusp-generated build/backup/audit scratch silently fills","summary":"Token-budget pre-flight gates exist for LLM spend but disk is treated as ambient infrastructure with no reservation or estimation step. No existing scheduler path reads free space before dispatch or uses historical run data to estimate scratch need. The reaper exists ad-hoc (gio trash, manual rm) but is not coupled to job dispatch as a blocking dependency.\n\n**Mechanism:** Add a disk-scratch pre-flight check to every job launcher: (1) query free bytes on /mnt/data before dispatch, (2) compare against a per-job-type scratch estimate derived from a rolling P90 of the last N completed runs of that job type (stored as a lightweight CSV alongside existing spend telemetry), (3) if free < estimate * safety_margin (e.g. 1.5x), block dispatch and synchronously invoke a scratch-reaper that deletes papercusp-generated build/backup/audit scratch ordered by age+type, then re-checks before retrying. Job never launches into a headroom deficit; ENOSPC becomes structurally impossible for scheduled jobs.\n\n**The bet:** Eliminates the entire class of mid-run ENOSPC failures for scheduled jobs, recovers phantom-regression investigation time, and creates a natural accounting surface that makes the 2.3TB accumulation visible and self-healing rather than requiring manual audits.\n\n**Cheap experiment (D-006):**\n- Hypothesis: A per-job-type P90 scratch estimate computed from the last 20 completed runs will predict actual peak scratch usage within 2x for ≥80% of job executions, making it a viable pre-flight gate threshold.\n- Method: Instrument three existing high-scratch job types (build, checkpoint, verify) to log (job_type, run_id, peak_scratch_bytes_delta) via df before and after for the next 20 runs each — no launcher changes, just a wrapper script. Compute P90 per type and compare to actuals.\n- Falsified if: If P90 estimate is off by more than 3x for more than 30% of runs (high variance makes estimation useless) OR if free-space snapshots show scratch is dominated by long-lived residue rather than per-run peaks (meaning estimation is the wrong lever and reaping alone suffices), the pre-flight gate mechanism is wrong.\n\n_Scout-generated. The worktree DIVERGED from the hive head with a content conflict on d055-conflict.txt and soak-heartbeat-joiner.txt. D-055 step 2 dispatched merge-resolver at 17:46:49Z and 17:56:08Z. Each run loaded (WI-10006694 fixed: no 'Dynamic require of'), connected PG, computed its prompt budget, then died: '[dbos-invoke] role=merge-resolver exited 127 with empty stderr'; stdout tail 'spawn error: spawn claude ENOENT'. Effect: a member without the claude CLI can never resolve a D-055 conflict. Its worktree stays diverged and cannot follow hive changes. The only trace is a 127 with empty stderr in serve.log; nothing in worktree_divergence or any owner-facing surface says the resolver cannot run.\n\nFix direction [inferred, not designed yet]: (1) preflight the resolver role's agent runtime before D-055 dispatch, and record resolverUnavailable {runtime, reason:'runtime-missing'} in routines.metadata.worktree_divergence so the divergence status says why; (2) do not re-dispatch a resolver that cannot spawn on every new path set; (3) decide whether a member may pick any installed runtime, or whether the conflict should escalate to the owner/hive integrator instead. Test: dispatch with a PATH lacking the runtime records resolverUnavailable and does not spawn.\n\nSoak-side: to exercise D-055 end to end, the GCP joiner needs an agent runtime plus a credential. So pull was NOT blocked; pullBlocked=1 was a probe artefact. Fix + tests in tree. (2) The S8 probe read the joiner worktree HEAD, which never integrated the tower files because the joiner had diverged. Fixed: on_joiner now reads all refs (p2p-join-soak.test.ts head-only mutant caught), installed by atomic rename at 18:12Z. Push WAS blocked: the tower never accepted the joiner after-revoke commit 2153d21 within 1200s. The data plane cut off too (outbox drain: no epoch key for epoch 4). Evidence: ~/.cache/agent-capacity/c1010r/{run.log,evidence/serve.log,evidence/routine-meta.txt}. papercusp-hosted-control-plane.service, which runs `tsx bin/serve.ts` from the canonical staging tree (WorkingDirectory apps/operator, PAPERCUSP_INTEGRATION_ROOT = canonical tree) and started 00:14:57Z; hosted-vm-size.ts, first-workspace.ts and hosted-browser.ts were last modified 21:45-21:46Z (TZ=UTC stat), so the running control plane loaded the server half [inferred from start time > mtime + tsx source load; not probed with a live request]. The cloud portal release live since 00:39:54Z carries the form (grep 'How many agents will it run' = 2 files in .portal-runtime/portal/current). Provisioning is enqueued to bg-host, which also contains the change (dev:pipeline_position containsChange=true).\nREAL BLOCKER: first-workspace is gated by PAPERCUSP_HOSTED_FIRST_WORKSPACE_ORGANIZATIONS (~/.config/papercusp/hosted-control-plane.env): 2 entries, no wildcard, so a fresh WorkOS test org from portal/e2e/hosted-test-user.ts is refused first_workspace_not_enabled, and the allowlisted orgs already hold a workspace (organization_already_has_workspace). Options: (a) add a dedicated test org to the allowlist + restart the hosted control plane, then a live spec using hosted-test-user.ts that POSTs expectedAgents=50 and checks gcloud machineType; (b) drive the provisioning layer directly with gcpFirstWorkspaceDesiredSpec({expectedAgents:50}) into the papercusp-hosted-workspaces project. Cleanup caveat: WI-10002751 (no hosted workspace delete). The tower then logged `[pot-git] serve OK repo=gh-1303510992 peer=ywj8zjsF id=1 code=0 1067ms` at 17:53:08Z (papercusp-bg-host journal). The joiner's refs/hive/staging = 1d4b5b4, the tower commit (17:50:07Z) that carries s8-tower-after-revoke.txt. In the same window presence-gossip REJECTED that signer ('not an attested device of this hive'), and the joiner's outbox drain fails with 'no epoch key for epoch 4' (the data-plane cut-off works).\n\nROOT CAUSE (code read): packages/operator-core/lib/sync/pot-git/serve-wiring.ts authorizes the verified peer device ONLY for scopes/-family repos (authorizeScopeRepoServe, WI-3641). For the pot's main repo there is only the topic gate, and a revoke changes neither the topic nor any serve-time check, so harness_shared.pot_members.revoked_pubkeys is never consulted on serve.\n\nFIX: before serving any repo, when the socket yields a signature-verified device pubkey, refuse 'revoked-device' if it is in loadRevokedHivePubkeysForLocalPot(cfg.workspaceId, cfg.potHomeSlug). Fail closed on a lookup error. Deny-list, not allow-list: whether a cold joiner's first fetch precedes its attestation landing on the tower is unmeasured, so an attested-only gate could break join. That hardening is a follow-up. Test in serve-wiring's suite: a revoked verified peer is refused; a non-revoked peer is served.\n\nCaveat: c1010k's non-revoked joiner was live on the same hive at the time (WI-10006817), but the tower's own serve log names ywj8zjsF as the peer it served, so the attribution does not rest on the joiner Observed 2026-10-08 ~03:36-03:46Z in consult fork session su-52bbe7ef-405c-4b48-b7ed-866fd4f62206 (fork of su-38b2a9fb, consult conv-muyzhey2-0000-cd9a9aea50d04d558d9155242069c6ae), serving build d740fc3802. In each of five consecutive parallel batches (4-13 calls: code:run + tools:invoke→capability:read / improvements:capture), 2-6 calls refused `authorization_denied: Kernel preflight denied tool \"code:run\" (or \"tools:invoke\"): current session authority could not be read; no effect authorized` while identical-shape sibling calls in the SAME batch succeeded; retrying a denied call later succeeded. The authority read flaps per call, not per session: a transient read failure is rendered as a hard DENY instead of resolver-failed/retry. Impact: read-only evidence gathering took ~3x the round-trips; an agent trusting the refusal would wrongly conclude it is unauthorized. Suggested probe: correlate preflight denials for this owner in that window with authority-read latency/error logs and harness_shared.tool_invocations. The re-binding pins the source as it was at bind time, ~9h55m after the run, after admin.ts changed.\n\nIMPACT: spec-test-adequacy's freshness criterion says to 'Use plans:get-spec-evidence currentness', so terminal card EI-25238849837016692 (subject …#AUTO-BAR-R-10-P-013@4) rates freshness PASS on a proof that never executed against the current admin.ts. Any rebind or supersedeAtRevision of an old run launders source staleness into 'current' without re-execution.\n\nPROPOSED FIX: currentness should compare the CURRENT source against the source state at the bound run's execution: the earliest pin recorded for that testRunId, or the run's commit_sha plus its dirty state. Alternatively, refuse to bind a testRunId whose execution predates a change in the pinned paths. Its observed rows show testRunId 20806130 was bound first at spec rev 3 with source pin 5ea8263a and later at rev 4 with source pin 8bdc4b1e; only the later pin reads current after admin.ts changed. The test execution predates both bindings. Current bind handler re-measures live files and writes a new measurement pin on each bind. `bindSpecEvidence` carries prior details only for the same spec revision and matching source fingerprint, so it cannot preserve the earlier run pin across a revision. `listSpecEvidence` re-measures current files and compares against each row's stored source fingerprint. This explains the source-currentness laundering mechanism.\nThe active sibling item EI-25194393482358454 concerns dirty test-run currentness; current classifier now makes dirty runs unknown. Its next action is separate. Inspected target files are clean; locks:queue showed no holds at that read. (su-a973d2b0, 2026-10-07 ~00:58Z, [self-imposed] design). Settled design (supersedes the datatype idea: natures are a fixed work|record|document|event set and membership is none of them):\n1. plugin-sdk provider.ts: ProviderDescriptor.membership?: { identityProvider } (validated in validateProviderDescriptor) + ProviderSyncPage.membership?: { members: [{ providerUserId }] } = the COMPLETE current membership; full replacement.\n2. connector-runtime.ts: assertPage refuses a membership the descriptor did not declare or a malformed one; the sync loop applies page.membership via a new deps.applyMembership BEFORE commitCursor (replay-safe: full replace is idempotent); outcome.membership reports it.\n3. datatype-destination-sink.ts: applyDataSourceMembership(sql, { workspaceId, sourceId, identityProvider, members }) -> organization scope + permission_mapping.mode source-acl only; ensurePermissionList(normalizePersonalSource(kind), source.id) (the same key corpusRouteFor/document sink use) + replacePermissionListMembers. Other scopes: { applied:false, reason }.\n4. github-issues plugin: 4th phase 'collaborators' (GET /repos/o/r/collaborators?affiliation=all, per_page, accumulate ids in cursor.members, emit on last page). 403/404 there = cannot enumerate -> emit EMPTY membership (fail-closed). Manifest membership.identityProvider='github' (GitHub numeric user id = OAuth identity idField id).\n5. Tests: github-issues-provider.integration.test.ts fake serves /collaborators; flip :656-659; member-can-read / non-member-cannot via mapProviderIdentity + searchOrganizationDocuments; page counts +1 per pass; plugin-sdk validator unit test.\n\n---\n⟦work-item-flight-record v1 begin⟧\ncaptured_at: 2026-10-07T01:10:37.790Z\ncause: forced-interrupt\nowner: su-a973d2b0-0677-4a5b-b483-93676730c9e9\nintent: WI-10006211: provider-declared org membership feed into document_permission_members (org-scope ticket-comment docs). (su-a973d2b0, 2026-10-07 ~00:58Z, [self-imposed] design). Settled design (supersedes the datatype idea: natures are a fixed work|record|document|event set and membership is none of them):\n1. plugin-sdk provider.ts: ProviderDescriptor.membership?: { identityProvider } (validated in validateProviderDescriptor) + ProviderSyncPage.membership?: { members: [{ providerUserId }] } = the COMPLETE current membership; full replacement.\n2. connector-runtime.ts: assertPage refuses a membership the descriptor did not declare or a malformed one; the sync loop applies page.membership via a new deps.applyMembership BEFORE commitCursor (replay-safe: full replace is idempotent); outcome.membership reports it.\n3. datatype-destination-sink.ts: applyDataSourceMembership(sql, { workspaceId, sourceId, identityProvider, members }) -> organization scope + permission_mapping.mode source-acl only; ensurePermissionList(normalizePersonalSource(kind), source.id) (the same key corpusRouteFor/document sink use) + replacePermissionListMembers. Other scopes: { applied:false, reason }.\n4. github-issues plugin: 4th phase 'collaborators' (GET /repos/o/r/collaborators?affiliation=all, per_page, accumulate ids in cursor.members, emit on last page). 403/404 there = cannot enumerate -> emit EMPTY membership (fail-closed). Manifest membership.identityProvider='github' (GitHub numeric user id = OAuth identity idField id).\n5. Tests: github-issues-provider.integration.test.ts fake serves /collaborators; flip :656-659; member-can-read / non-member-cannot via mapProviderIdentity + searchOrganizationDocuments; page counts +1 per pass; plugin-sdk validator unit test.\n\n---\n⟦work-item-flight-record v1 begin⟧\ncaptured_at: 2026-10-07T01:10:37.790Z\ncause: forced-interrupt\nowner: su-a973d2b0-0677-4a5b-b483-93676730c9e9\nintent: WI-10006211: provider-declared org membership feed into document_permission_members (org-scope ticket-comment docs). (su-a973d2b0, 2026-10-07 ~00:58Z, [self-imposed] design). Settled design (supersedes the datatype idea: natures are a fixed work|record|document|event set and membership is none of them):\n1. plugin-sdk provider.ts: ProviderDescriptor.membership?: { identityProvider } (validated in validateProviderDescriptor) + ProviderSyncPage.membership?: { members: [{ providerUserId }] } = the COMPLETE current membership; full replacement.\n2. connector-runtime.ts: assertPage refuses a membership the descriptor did not declare or a malformed one; the sync loop applies page.membership via a new deps.applyMembership BEFORE commitCursor (replay-safe: full replace is idempotent); outcome.membership reports it.\n3. datatype-destination-sink.ts: applyDataSourceMembership(sql, { workspaceId, sourceId, identityProvider, members }) -> organization scope + permission_mapping.mode source-acl only; ensurePermissionList(normalizePersonalSource(kind), source.id) (the same key corpusRouteFor/document sink use) + replacePermissionListMembers. Other scopes: { applied:false, reason }.\n4. github-issues plugin: 4th phase 'collaborators' (GET /repos/o/r/collaborators?affiliation=all, per_page, accumulate ids in cursor.members, emit on last page). 403/404 there = cannot enumerate -> emit EMPTY membership (fail-closed). Manifest membership.identityProvider='github' (GitHub numeric user id = OAuth identity idField id).\n5. Tests: github-issues-provider.integration.test.ts fake serves /collaborators; flip :656-659; member-can-read / non-member-cannot via mapProviderIdentity + searchOrganizationDocuments; page counts +1 per pass; plugin-sdk validator unit test.\n\n---\n⟦work-item-flight-record v1 begin⟧\ncaptured_at: 2026-10-07T01:10:37.790Z\ncause: forced-interrupt\nowner: su-a973d2b0-0677-4a5b-b483-93676730c9e9\nintent: WI-10006211: provider-declared org membership feed into document_permission_members (org-scope ticket-comment docs).
